                                                                                                                      EVM's 3-Phase DUAL PMIC Power Distribution Network (PDN)

                                                                                                                                                                                  (3-Phase Buck supplying VDD_CPU)
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       "PCB Notes:
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       For multi-phase Buck converter configs, route
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       remote sense feedback as follows:
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          1. Pseudo differential pair traces on same layer
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       & next to primarily power plane segment. Avoid
D                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      routing near to any noisy/switching signals.                                                                   D

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          2. Connect each trace, as close as possible, to
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       power & Gnd vias or across Dcap in middle of SOC
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       power ball group.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          3. Trace widths = 4-8mil & separation distance =
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       8-50mil, try to keep traces near each other as best
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       as possible while

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        For single-phase Buck converters, route remote
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       sense feedback as follows:
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          1. Single-ended traces on same layer & next to
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       primarily power plane segment as best as possible.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       Avoid routing near to any noisy/switching signals.


                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            PMIC- A
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  VSYS_3V3                                                                                                                2. Connect each trace, as close as possible, to
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       a power via near in middle of SOC power ball group.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          3. Trace widths = 4-8mil"

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        For multi-phase Buck converter configs, route remote
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        sense feedback as follows:
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           1. Pseudo differential pair traces on same layer
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        & next to primarily power plane segment. Avoid
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        routing near to any noisy/switching signals.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           2. Connect each trace, as close as possible, to
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        power & Gnd vias or across Dcap in middle of SOC
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        power ball group.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           3. Trace widths = 4-8mil & separation distance =
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        8-50mil, try to keep traces near each other as best
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        as possible while
D                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      D
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         For single-phase Buck converters, route remote
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        sense feedback as follows:

                                                                                                                                                   PMIC - B
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           1. Single-ended traces on same layer & next to
                                                                                                         VSYS_3V3                                                                                                                                                                                       primarily power plane segment as best as possible.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        Avoid routing near to any noisy/switching signals.
                                                                             VIO_VIN                                                                                                                                                                                                                       2. Connect each trace, as close as possible, to a
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        power via near in middle of SOC power ball group.
                                                                                                                     C41        C38                                                                                                                                                                        3. Trace widths = 4-8mil"
